-    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);
-    
-    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);
-    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);
-    const double discount = exp(-r * T);
-    
-    double price;
-    if (option.type == CALL) {
-        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;
-    } else { // PUT
-        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
+// One thread prices one option in the common case.  The grid-stride loop also
+// supports data sets larger than the device's maximum one-dimensional grid.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(kThreadsPerBlock)
+void blackScholesKernel(const double* __restrict__ inputs,
+                        double* __restrict__ prices,
+                        const std::size_t numOptions) {
+    const double* const signs = inputs + SIGN * numOptions;
+    const double* const strikes = inputs + STRIKE * numOptions;
+    const double* const spots = inputs + SPOT * numOptions;
+    const double* const dividendYields = inputs + DIVIDEND_YIELD * numOptions;
+    const double* const riskFreeRates = inputs + RISK_FREE_RATE * numOptions;
+    const double* const times = inputs + TIME_TO_MATURITY * numOptions;
+    const double* const volatilities = inputs + VOLATILITY * numOptions;
+
+    const std::size_t first =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const std::size_t stride =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;
+
+    for (std::size_t i = first; i < numOptions; i += stride) {
+        const double time = times[i];
+        const double volatility = volatilities[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || volatility <= 0.0) {
+            prices[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double spot = spots[i];
+        const double strike = strikes[i];
+        const double dividendYield = dividendYields[i];
+        const double riskFreeRate = riskFreeRates[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double volatilitySqrtTime = volatility * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(spot / strike) +
+             (riskFreeRate - dividendYield +
+              0.5 * volatility * volatility) *
+                 time) /
+            volatilitySqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - volatilitySqrtTime;
+
+        // sign is +1 for calls and -1 for puts.  This form removes divergent
+        // call/put branches while evaluating the same two normal CDFs.
+        const double sign = signs[i];
+        const double discountedSpot = spot * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+        const double discountedStrike = strike * exp(-riskFreeRate * time);
+        prices[i] =
+            sign * (discountedSpot * cumulativeNormalDevice(sign * d1) -
+                    discountedStrike * cumulativeNormalDevice(sign * d2));
     }
-    
-    return price;
 }

+// Convert the host-friendly array of structures to a device-friendly
+// structure of arrays.  Every warp consequently issues coalesced loads for
+// each field used by the pricing kernel.
+void packOptions(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,
+                 std::vector<double>& packedOptions) {
+    const std::size_t numOptions = options.size();
+    packedOptions.resize(kInputFieldCount * numOptions);
+
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
+        const OptionInput& option = options[i];
+        packedOptions[SIGN * numOptions + i] =
+            option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+        packedOptions[STRIKE * numOptions + i] = option.strike;
+        packedOptions[SPOT * numOptions + i] = option.spot;
+        packedOptions[DIVIDEND_YIELD * numOptions + i] = option.q;
+        packedOptions[RISK_FREE_RATE * numOptions + i] = option.r;
+        packedOptions[TIME_TO_MATURITY * numOptions + i] = option.t;
+        packedOptions[VOLATILITY * numOptions + i] = option.vol;
+    }
+}
+
+float priceOptionsCuda(const std::vector<double>& packedOptions,
+                       std::vector<double>& results) {
+    const std::size_t numOptions = results.size();
+    if (numOptions == 0) {
+        return 0.0F;
+    }
+
+    // Inputs and outputs share one allocation to minimize CUDA allocation
+    // overhead while retaining separately aligned contiguous regions.
+    DeviceBuffer<double> deviceStorage(packedOptions.size() + numOptions);
+    double* const deviceInputs = deviceStorage.get();
+    double* const deviceResults = deviceInputs + packedOptions.size();
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceInputs, packedOptions.data(),
+                         packedOptions.size() * sizeof(double),
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+              "input transfer");
+
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDevice(&device), "device query");
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device),
+              "device-properties query");
+
+    const std::size_t requiredBlocks =
+        numOptions / kThreadsPerBlock +
+        (numOptions % kThreadsPerBlock != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+    const std::size_t maxBlocks =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(properties.maxGridSize[0]);
+    const int blockCount =
+        static_cast<int>(std::min(requiredBlocks, maxBlocks));
+
+    // Force lazy module loading before the timed region so the reported
+    // computation time measures kernel work rather than one-time setup.
+    cudaFuncAttributes kernelAttributes{};
+    cudaCheck(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&kernelAttributes, blackScholesKernel),
+              "pricing-kernel initialization");
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start-event creation");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop-event creation");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(start), "start-event recording");
+
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blockCount, kThreadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceInputs, deviceResults, numOptions);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "pricing-kernel launch");
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(stop), "stop-event recording");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "pricing-kernel execution");
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop),
+              "kernel timing");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(start), "start-event destruction");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "stop-event destruction");
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                         numOptions * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "result transfer");
+    return elapsedMilliseconds;
+}

+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
+        const OptionInput& option = options[i];
+        packedOptions[SIGN * numOptions + i] =
+            option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+        packedOptions[STRIKE * numOptions + i] = option.strike;
+        packedOptions[SPOT * numOptions + i] = option.spot;
+        packedOptions[DIVIDEND_YIELD * numOptions + i] = option.q;
+        packedOptions[RISK_FREE_RATE * numOptions + i] = option.r;
+        packedOptions[TIME_TO_MATURITY * numOptions + i] = option.t;
+        packedOptions[VOLATILITY * numOptions + i] = option.vol;
+    }
+}
+
+float priceOptionsCuda(const std::vector<double>& packedOptions,
+                       std::vector<double>& results) {
+    const std::size_t numOptions = results.size();
+    if (numOptions == 0) {
+        return 0.0F;
+    }
+
+    // Inputs and outputs share one allocation to minimize CUDA allocation
+    // overhead while retaining separately aligned contiguous regions.
+    DeviceBuffer<double> deviceStorage(packedOptions.size() + numOptions);
+    double* const deviceInputs = deviceStorage.get();
+    double* const deviceResults = deviceInputs + packedOptions.size();
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceInputs, packedOptions.data(),
+                         packedOptions.size() * sizeof(double),
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+              "input transfer");
+
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDevice(&device), "device query");
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device),
+              "device-properties query");
+
+    const std::size_t requiredBlocks =
+        numOptions / kThreadsPerBlock +
+        (numOptions % kThreadsPerBlock != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+    const std::size_t maxBlocks =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(properties.maxGridSize[0]);
+    const int blockCount =
+        static_cast<int>(std::min(requiredBlocks, maxBlocks));
+
+    // Force lazy module loading before the timed region so the reported
+    // computation time measures kernel work rather than one-time setup.
+    cudaFuncAttributes kernelAttributes{};
+    cudaCheck(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&kernelAttributes, blackScholesKernel),
+              "pricing-kernel initialization");
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start-event creation");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop-event creation");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(start), "start-event recording");
+
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blockCount, kThreadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceInputs, deviceResults, numOptions);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "pricing-kernel launch");
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(stop), "stop-event recording");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "pricing-kernel execution");
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop),
+              "kernel timing");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(start), "start-event destruction");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "stop-event destruction");
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                         numOptions * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "result transfer");
+    return elapsedMilliseconds;
+}

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);

+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const std::size_t count) {
+        if (count != 0) {
+            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 count * sizeof(T)),
+                      "device allocation");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    [[nodiscard]] T* get() noexcept { return data_; }
+    [[nodiscard]] const T* get() const noexcept { return data_; }
+
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+// Standard normal cumulative distribution function.  Keeping this operation
+// in double precision preserves the numerical behavior of the CPU benchmark.
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * kInvSqrtTwo));
 }
 
-// Black-Scholes formula for European options
-double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {
-    const double S = option.spot;
-    const double K = option.strike;
-    const double r = option.r;
-    const double q = option.q;
-    const double T = option.t;

-    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
-        return 0.0;
-    }
-    
-    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));
-    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);
-    
-    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);
-    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);
-    const double discount = exp(-r * T);
-    
-    double price;
-    if (option.type == CALL) {
-        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;
-    } else { // PUT
-        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
+// One thread prices one option in the common case.  The grid-stride loop also
+// supports data sets larger than the device's maximum one-dimensional grid.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(kThreadsPerBlock)
+void blackScholesKernel(const double* __restrict__ inputs,
+                        double* __restrict__ prices,
+                        const std::size_t numOptions) {
+    const double* const signs = inputs + SIGN * numOptions;
+    const double* const strikes = inputs + STRIKE * numOptions;
+    const double* const spots = inputs + SPOT * numOptions;
+    const double* const dividendYields = inputs + DIVIDEND_YIELD * numOptions;
+    const double* const riskFreeRates = inputs + RISK_FREE_RATE * numOptions;
+    const double* const times = inputs + TIME_TO_MATURITY * numOptions;
+    const double* const volatilities = inputs + VOLATILITY * numOptions;
+
+    const std::size_t first =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const std::size_t stride =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;
+
+    for (std::size_t i = first; i < numOptions; i += stride) {
+        const double time = times[i];
+        const double volatility = volatilities[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || volatility <= 0.0) {
+            prices[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double spot = spots[i];
+        const double strike = strikes[i];
+        const double dividendYield = dividendYields[i];
+        const double riskFreeRate = riskFreeRates[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double volatilitySqrtTime = volatility * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(spot / strike) +
+             (riskFreeRate - dividendYield +
+              0.5 * volatility * volatility) *
+                 time) /
+            volatilitySqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - volatilitySqrtTime;
+
+        // sign is +1 for calls and -1 for puts.  This form removes divergent
+        // call/put branches while evaluating the same two normal CDFs.
+        const double sign = signs[i];
+        const double discountedSpot = spot * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+        const double discountedStrike = strike * exp(-riskFreeRate * time);
+        prices[i] =
+            sign * (discountedSpot * cumulativeNormalDevice(sign * d1) -
+                    discountedStrike * cumulativeNormalDevice(sign * d2));
     }
-    
-    return price;
 }

+// Convert the host-friendly array of structures to a device-friendly
+// structure of arrays.  Every warp consequently issues coalesced loads for
+// each field used by the pricing kernel.
+void packOptions(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,
+                 std::vector<double>& packedOptions) {
+    const std::size_t numOptions = options.size();
+    packedOptions.resize(kInputFieldCount * numOptions);
+
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
+        const OptionInput& option = options[i];
+        packedOptions[SIGN * numOptions + i] =
+            option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+        packedOptions[STRIKE * numOptions + i] = option.strike;
+        packedOptions[SPOT * numOptions + i] = option.spot;
+        packedOptions[DIVIDEND_YIELD * numOptions + i] = option.q;
+        packedOptions[RISK_FREE_RATE * numOptions + i] = option.r;
+        packedOptions[TIME_TO_MATURITY * numOptions + i] = option.t;
+        packedOptions[VOLATILITY * numOptions + i] = option.vol;
+    }
+}
+
+float priceOptionsCuda(const std::vector<double>& packedOptions,
+                       std::vector<double>& results) {
+    const std::size_t numOptions = results.size();
+    if (numOptions == 0) {
+        return 0.0F;
+    }
+
+    // Inputs and outputs share one allocation to minimize CUDA allocation
+    // overhead while retaining separately aligned contiguous regions.
+    DeviceBuffer<double> deviceStorage(packedOptions.size() + numOptions);
+    double* const deviceInputs = deviceStorage.get();
+    double* const deviceResults = deviceInputs + packedOptions.size();
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceInputs, packedOptions.data(),
+                         packedOptions.size() * sizeof(double),
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+              "input transfer");
+
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDevice(&device), "device query");
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device),
+              "device-properties query");
+
+    const std::size_t requiredBlocks =
+        numOptions / kThreadsPerBlock +
+        (numOptions % kThreadsPerBlock != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+    const std::size_t maxBlocks =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(properties.maxGridSize[0]);
+    const int blockCount =
+        static_cast<int>(std::min(requiredBlocks, maxBlocks));
+
+    // Force lazy module loading before the timed region so the reported
+    // computation time measures kernel work rather than one-time setup.
+    cudaFuncAttributes kernelAttributes{};
+    cudaCheck(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&kernelAttributes, blackScholesKernel),
+              "pricing-kernel initialization");
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start-event creation");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop-event creation");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(start), "start-event recording");
+
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blockCount, kThreadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceInputs, deviceResults, numOptions);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "pricing-kernel launch");
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(stop), "stop-event recording");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "pricing-kernel execution");
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop),
+              "kernel timing");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(start), "start-event destruction");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "stop-event destruction");
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                         numOptions * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "result transfer");
+    return elapsedMilliseconds;
+}

+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const std::size_t count) {
+        if (count != 0) {
+            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 count * sizeof(T)),

+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {

+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;

+    [[nodiscard]] const T* get() const noexcept { return data_; }

+// Standard normal cumulative distribution function.  Keeping this operation
+// in double precision preserves the numerical behavior of the CPU benchmark.
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * kInvSqrtTwo));

-        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
+// One thread prices one option in the common case.  The grid-stride loop also
+// supports data sets larger than the device's maximum one-dimensional grid.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(kThreadsPerBlock)
+void blackScholesKernel(const double* __restrict__ inputs,
+                        double* __restrict__ prices,
+                        const std::size_t numOptions) {
+    const double* const signs = inputs + SIGN * numOptions;
+    const double* const strikes = inputs + STRIKE * numOptions;
+    const double* const spots = inputs + SPOT * numOptions;
+    const double* const dividendYields = inputs + DIVIDEND_YIELD * numOptions;
+    const double* const riskFreeRates = inputs + RISK_FREE_RATE * numOptions;
+    const double* const times = inputs + TIME_TO_MATURITY * numOptions;
+    const double* const volatilities = inputs + VOLATILITY * numOptions;
+
+    const std::size_t first =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const std::size_t stride =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;
+
+    for (std::size_t i = first; i < numOptions; i += stride) {
+        const double time = times[i];
+        const double volatility = volatilities[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || volatility <= 0.0) {
+            prices[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double spot = spots[i];
+        const double strike = strikes[i];
+        const double dividendYield = dividendYields[i];
+        const double riskFreeRate = riskFreeRates[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double volatilitySqrtTime = volatility * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(spot / strike) +
+             (riskFreeRate - dividendYield +
+              0.5 * volatility * volatility) *
+                 time) /
+            volatilitySqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - volatilitySqrtTime;
+
+        // sign is +1 for calls and -1 for puts.  This form removes divergent
+        // call/put branches while evaluating the same two normal CDFs.
+        const double sign = signs[i];
+        const double discountedSpot = spot * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+        const double discountedStrike = strike * exp(-riskFreeRate * time);
+        prices[i] =
+            sign * (discountedSpot * cumulativeNormalDevice(sign * d1) -
+                    discountedStrike * cumulativeNormalDevice(sign * d2));
     }
-    
-    return price;
 }

+// Convert the host-friendly array of structures to a device-friendly
+// structure of arrays.  Every warp consequently issues coalesced loads for
+// each field used by the pricing kernel.
+void packOptions(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,
+                 std::vector<double>& packedOptions) {
+    const std::size_t numOptions = options.size();
+    packedOptions.resize(kInputFieldCount * numOptions);
+
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
+        const OptionInput& option = options[i];
+        packedOptions[SIGN * numOptions + i] =
+            option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+        packedOptions[STRIKE * numOptions + i] = option.strike;
+        packedOptions[SPOT * numOptions + i] = option.spot;
+        packedOptions[DIVIDEND_YIELD * numOptions + i] = option.q;
+        packedOptions[RISK_FREE_RATE * numOptions + i] = option.r;
+        packedOptions[TIME_TO_MATURITY * numOptions + i] = option.t;
+        packedOptions[VOLATILITY * numOptions + i] = option.vol;
+    }
+}
+
+float priceOptionsCuda(const std::vector<double>& packedOptions,
+                       std::vector<double>& results) {
+    const std::size_t numOptions = results.size();
+    if (numOptions == 0) {
+        return 0.0F;
+    }
+
+    // Inputs and outputs share one allocation to minimize CUDA allocation
+    // overhead while retaining separately aligned contiguous regions.
+    DeviceBuffer<double> deviceStorage(packedOptions.size() + numOptions);
+    double* const deviceInputs = deviceStorage.get();
+    double* const deviceResults = deviceInputs + packedOptions.size();
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceInputs, packedOptions.data(),
+                         packedOptions.size() * sizeof(double),
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+              "input transfer");
+
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDevice(&device), "device query");
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device),
+              "device-properties query");
+
+    const std::size_t requiredBlocks =
+        numOptions / kThreadsPerBlock +
+        (numOptions % kThreadsPerBlock != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+    const std::size_t maxBlocks =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(properties.maxGridSize[0]);
+    const int blockCount =
+        static_cast<int>(std::min(requiredBlocks, maxBlocks));
+
+    // Force lazy module loading before the timed region so the reported
+    // computation time measures kernel work rather than one-time setup.
+    cudaFuncAttributes kernelAttributes{};
+    cudaCheck(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&kernelAttributes, blackScholesKernel),
+              "pricing-kernel initialization");
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start-event creation");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop-event creation");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(start), "start-event recording");
+
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blockCount, kThreadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceInputs, deviceResults, numOptions);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "pricing-kernel launch");
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(stop), "stop-event recording");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "pricing-kernel execution");
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop),
+              "kernel timing");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(start), "start-event destruction");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "stop-event destruction");
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                         numOptions * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "result transfer");
+    return elapsedMilliseconds;
+}

The fresh CUDA build passes validation at `-n 0`, `1`, `257`, `10,000`, and `1,000,000`. Its result statistics match the CPU reference to floating-point precision (the 10,000-option sum differs by about \(9.3\times10^{-10}\), with matching printed extrema), and on the available RTX 3090 the timed kernel measured about 0.040 ms for 10,000 options and 0.864 ms for 1,000,000. I’m doing a final source/build audit now.

+// Convert the host-friendly array of structures to a device-friendly
+// structure of arrays.  Every warp consequently issues coalesced loads for
+// each field used by the pricing kernel.
+void packOptions(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,
+                 std::vector<double>& packedOptions) {
+    const std::size_t numOptions = options.size();
+    packedOptions.resize(kInputFieldCount * numOptions);
+
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
+        const OptionInput& option = options[i];
+        packedOptions[SIGN * numOptions + i] =
+            option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+        packedOptions[STRIKE * numOptions + i] = option.strike;
+        packedOptions[SPOT * numOptions + i] = option.spot;
+        packedOptions[DIVIDEND_YIELD * numOptions + i] = option.q;
+        packedOptions[RISK_FREE_RATE * numOptions + i] = option.r;
+        packedOptions[TIME_TO_MATURITY * numOptions + i] = option.t;
+        packedOptions[VOLATILITY * numOptions + i] = option.vol;
+    }
+}
+
+float priceOptionsCuda(const std::vector<double>& packedOptions,
+                       std::vector<double>& results) {
+    const std::size_t numOptions = results.size();
+    if (numOptions == 0) {
+        return 0.0F;
+    }
+
+    // Inputs and outputs share one allocation to minimize CUDA allocation
+    // overhead while retaining separately aligned contiguous regions.
+    DeviceBuffer<double> deviceStorage(packedOptions.size() + numOptions);
+    double* const deviceInputs = deviceStorage.get();
+    double* const deviceResults = deviceInputs + packedOptions.size();
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceInputs, packedOptions.data(),
+                         packedOptions.size() * sizeof(double),
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+              "input transfer");
+
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDevice(&device), "device query");
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device),
+              "device-properties query");
+
+    const std::size_t requiredBlocks =
+        numOptions / kThreadsPerBlock +
+        (numOptions % kThreadsPerBlock != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+    const std::size_t maxBlocks =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(properties.maxGridSize[0]);
+    const int blockCount =
+        static_cast<int>(std::min(requiredBlocks, maxBlocks));
+
+    // Force lazy module loading before the timed region so the reported
+    // computation time measures kernel work rather than one-time setup.
+    cudaFuncAttributes kernelAttributes{};
+    cudaCheck(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&kernelAttributes, blackScholesKernel),
+              "pricing-kernel initialization");
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start-event creation");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop-event creation");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(start), "start-event recording");
+
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blockCount, kThreadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceInputs, deviceResults, numOptions);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "pricing-kernel launch");
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(stop), "stop-event recording");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "pricing-kernel execution");
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop),
+              "kernel timing");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(start), "start-event destruction");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "stop-event destruction");
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                         numOptions * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "result transfer");
+    return elapsedMilliseconds;
+}

+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const std::size_t count) {
+        if (count != 0) {
+            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 count * sizeof(T)),

+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {

+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+

+    [[nodiscard]] const T* get() const noexcept { return data_; }

+private:

+// Standard normal cumulative distribution function.  Keeping this operation
+// in double precision preserves the numerical behavior of the CPU benchmark.
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {

-// Black-Scholes formula for European options
-double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {

-    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);
-    const double discount = exp(-r * T);
-    
-    double price;
-    if (option.type == CALL) {
-        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;
-    } else { // PUT
-        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
+// One thread prices one option in the common case.  The grid-stride loop also
+// supports data sets larger than the device's maximum one-dimensional grid.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(kThreadsPerBlock)
+void blackScholesKernel(const double* __restrict__ inputs,
+                        double* __restrict__ prices,
+                        const std::size_t numOptions) {
+    const double* const signs = inputs + SIGN * numOptions;
+    const double* const strikes = inputs + STRIKE * numOptions;
+    const double* const spots = inputs + SPOT * numOptions;
+    const double* const dividendYields = inputs + DIVIDEND_YIELD * numOptions;
+    const double* const riskFreeRates = inputs + RISK_FREE_RATE * numOptions;
+    const double* const times = inputs + TIME_TO_MATURITY * numOptions;
+    const double* const volatilities = inputs + VOLATILITY * numOptions;
+
+    const std::size_t first =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const std::size_t stride =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;
+
+    for (std::size_t i = first; i < numOptions; i += stride) {
+        const double time = times[i];
+        const double volatility = volatilities[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || volatility <= 0.0) {
+            prices[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double spot = spots[i];
+        const double strike = strikes[i];
+        const double dividendYield = dividendYields[i];
+        const double riskFreeRate = riskFreeRates[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double volatilitySqrtTime = volatility * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(spot / strike) +
+             (riskFreeRate - dividendYield +
+              0.5 * volatility * volatility) *
+                 time) /
+            volatilitySqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - volatilitySqrtTime;
+
+        // sign is +1 for calls and -1 for puts.  This form removes divergent
+        // call/put branches while evaluating the same two normal CDFs.
+        const double sign = signs[i];
+        const double discountedSpot = spot * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+        const double discountedStrike = strike * exp(-riskFreeRate * time);
+        prices[i] =
+            sign * (discountedSpot * cumulativeNormalDevice(sign * d1) -
+                    discountedStrike * cumulativeNormalDevice(sign * d2));
     }
-    
-    return price;
 }

   107	
   108	    for (std::size_t i = first; i < numOptions; i += stride) {
   109	        const double time = times[i];
   110	        const double volatility = volatilities[i];
   111	
   112	        if (time <= 0.0 || volatility <= 0.0) {
   113	            prices[i] = 0.0;
   114	            continue;
   115	        }
   116	
   117	        const double spot = spots[i];
   118	        const double strike = strikes[i];
   119	        const double dividendYield = dividendYields[i];
   120	        const double riskFreeRate = riskFreeRates[i];
   121	        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
   122	        const double volatilitySqrtTime = volatility * sqrtTime;
   123	        const double d1 =
   124	            (log(spot / strike) +
   125	             (riskFreeRate - dividendYield +
   126	              0.5 * volatility * volatility) *
   127	                 time) /
   128	            volatilitySqrtTime;
   129	        const double d2 = d1 - volatilitySqrtTime;
   130	
   131	        // sign is +1 for calls and -1 for puts.  This form removes divergent
   132	        // call/put branches while evaluating the same two normal CDFs.
   133	        const double sign = signs[i];
   134	        const double discountedSpot = spot * exp(-dividendYield * time);
   135	        const double discountedStrike = strike * exp(-riskFreeRate * time);
   136	        prices[i] =
   137	            sign * (discountedSpot * cumulativeNormalDevice(sign * d1) -
   138	                    discountedStrike * cumulativeNormalDevice(sign * d2));
   139	    }
   140	}
   141	
   142	}  // namespace
   143	
   144	// Standard test cases for validation
   145	inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
   146	    return {{
   147	        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},
   148	        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},
   149	        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
   150	        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},
   151	        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},
   152	        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
   153	        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},
   154	    }};
   155	}
   156	
   157	// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set
   158	void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {
   159	    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
   160	    options.resize(numOptions);
   161	    
   162	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
   163	        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
   164	        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];
   165	        options[i] = base;
   166	        
   167	        // Add some variation for larger datasets
   168	        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
   169	        options[i].spot *= factor;
   170	        options[i].strike *= factor;
   171	    }
   172	}
   173	
   174	// Convert the host-friendly array of structures to a device-friendly
   175	// structure of arrays.  Every warp consequently issues coalesced loads for
   176	// each field used by the pricing kernel.
   177	void packOptions(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,
   178	                 std::vector<double>& packedOptions) {
   179	    const std::size_t numOptions = options.size();
   180	    packedOptions.resize(kInputFieldCount * numOptions);
   181	
   182	    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
   183	        const OptionInput& option = options[i];
   184	        packedOptions[SIGN * numOptions + i] =
   185	            option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
   186	        packedOptions[STRIKE * numOptions + i] = option.strike;
   187	        packedOptions[SPOT * numOptions + i] = option.spot;
   188	        packedOptions[DIVIDEND_YIELD * numOptions + i] = option.q;
   189	        packedOptions[RISK_FREE_RATE * numOptions + i] = option.r;
   190	        packedOptions[TIME_TO_MATURITY * numOptions + i] = option.t;
   191	        packedOptions[VOLATILITY * numOptions + i] = option.vol;
   192	    }
   193	}
   194	
   195	float priceOptionsCuda(const std::vector<double>& packedOptions,
   196	                       std::vector<double>& results) {
   197	    const std::size_t numOptions = results.size();
   198	    if (numOptions == 0) {
   199	        return 0.0F;
   200	    }
   201	
   202	    // Inputs and outputs share one allocation to minimize CUDA allocation
   203	    // overhead while retaining separately aligned contiguous regions.
   204	    DeviceBuffer<double> deviceStorage(packedOptions.size() + numOptions);
   205	    double* const deviceInputs = deviceStorage.get();
   206	    double* const deviceResults = deviceInputs + packedOptions.size();
   207	
   208	    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceInputs, packedOptions.data(),
   209	                         packedOptions.size() * sizeof(double),
   210	                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
   211	              "input transfer");
   212	
   213	    int device = 0;
   214	    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
   215	    cudaCheck(cudaGetDevice(&device), "device query");
   216	    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device),
   217	              "device-properties query");
   218	
   219	    const std::size_t requiredBlocks =
   220	        numOptions / kThreadsPerBlock +
   221	        (numOptions % kThreadsPerBlock != 0 ? 1 : 0);
   222	    const std::size_t maxBlocks =
   223	        static_cast<std::size_t>(properties.maxGridSize[0]);
   224	    const int blockCount =
   225	        static_cast<int>(std::min(requiredBlocks, maxBlocks));
   226	
   227	    // Force lazy module loading before the timed region so the reported
   228	    // computation time measures kernel work rather than one-time setup.
   229	    cudaFuncAttributes kernelAttributes{};
   230	    cudaCheck(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&kernelAttributes, blackScholesKernel),
   231	              "pricing-kernel initialization");
   232	
   233	    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
   234	    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
   235	    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start-event creation");
   236	    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop-event creation");
   237	    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(start), "start-event recording");
   238	
   239	    blackScholesKernel<<<blockCount, kThreadsPerBlock>>>(
   240	        deviceInputs, deviceResults, numOptions);
   241	    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "pricing-kernel launch");
   242	
   243	    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(stop), "stop-event recording");
   244	    cudaCheck(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "pricing-kernel execution");
   245	
   246	    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
   247	    cudaCheck(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop),
   248	              "kernel timing");
   249	    cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(start), "start-event destruction");
   250	    cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "stop-event destruction");
   251	
   252	    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
   253	                         numOptions * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
   254	              "result transfer");
   255	    return elapsedMilliseconds;
   256	}
   257	
   258	bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
   259	                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
   260	    bool allPassed = true;
   261	    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());
   262	    
   263	    printf("Checking computed option prices:\n");
   264	    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {
   265	        const double computed = results[i];
   266	        const double expected = options[i].value;
   267	        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);
   268	        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);
   269	        
   270	        printf("  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\n", 
   271	               i, computed, expected, relError);
   272	        
   273	        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable
   274	        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {
   275	            printf("Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\n", i, computed);
   276	            allPassed = false;
   277	        }
   278	    }
   279	    
   280	    return allPassed;
   281	}
   282	
   283	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   284	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   285	    printf("Options:\n");
   286	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\n");
   287	    printf("  -v           Enable validation against known values\n");
   288	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   289	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   290	}

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(status));

+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const std::size_t count) {
+        if (count != 0) {
+            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 count * sizeof(T)),
+                      "device allocation");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    [[nodiscard]] T* get() noexcept { return data_; }
+    [[nodiscard]] const T* get() const noexcept { return data_; }
+
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};

+// Standard normal cumulative distribution function.  Keeping this operation
+// in double precision preserves the numerical behavior of the CPU benchmark.
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * kInvSqrtTwo));

+// One thread prices one option in the common case.  The grid-stride loop also
+// supports data sets larger than the device's maximum one-dimensional grid.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(kThreadsPerBlock)
+void blackScholesKernel(const double* __restrict__ inputs,
+                        double* __restrict__ prices,
+                        const std::size_t numOptions) {
+    const double* const signs = inputs + SIGN * numOptions;
+    const double* const strikes = inputs + STRIKE * numOptions;
+    const double* const spots = inputs + SPOT * numOptions;
+    const double* const dividendYields = inputs + DIVIDEND_YIELD * numOptions;
+    const double* const riskFreeRates = inputs + RISK_FREE_RATE * numOptions;
+    const double* const times = inputs + TIME_TO_MATURITY * numOptions;
+    const double* const volatilities = inputs + VOLATILITY * numOptions;
+
+    const std::size_t first =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const std::size_t stride =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;
+
+    for (std::size_t i = first; i < numOptions; i += stride) {
+        const double time = times[i];
+        const double volatility = volatilities[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || volatility <= 0.0) {
+            prices[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double spot = spots[i];
+        const double strike = strikes[i];
+        const double dividendYield = dividendYields[i];
+        const double riskFreeRate = riskFreeRates[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double volatilitySqrtTime = volatility * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(spot / strike) +
+             (riskFreeRate - dividendYield +
+              0.5 * volatility * volatility) *
+                 time) /
+            volatilitySqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - volatilitySqrtTime;
+

+        // call/put branches while evaluating the same two normal CDFs.
+        const double sign = signs[i];
+        const double discountedSpot = spot * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+        const double discountedStrike = strike * exp(-riskFreeRate * time);
+        prices[i] =
+            sign * (discountedSpot * cumulativeNormalDevice(sign * d1) -
+                    discountedStrike * cumulativeNormalDevice(sign * d2));
     }
-    
-    return price;
 }

+// Convert the host-friendly array of structures to a device-friendly
+// structure of arrays.  Every warp consequently issues coalesced loads for
+// each field used by the pricing kernel.
+void packOptions(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,
+                 std::vector<double>& packedOptions) {
+    const std::size_t numOptions = options.size();
+    packedOptions.resize(kInputFieldCount * numOptions);
+
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
+        const OptionInput& option = options[i];
+        packedOptions[SIGN * numOptions + i] =
+            option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+        packedOptions[STRIKE * numOptions + i] = option.strike;
+        packedOptions[SPOT * numOptions + i] = option.spot;
+        packedOptions[DIVIDEND_YIELD * numOptions + i] = option.q;
+        packedOptions[RISK_FREE_RATE * numOptions + i] = option.r;
+        packedOptions[TIME_TO_MATURITY * numOptions + i] = option.t;
+        packedOptions[VOLATILITY * numOptions + i] = option.vol;

+float priceOptionsCuda(const std::vector<double>& packedOptions,
+                       std::vector<double>& results) {
+    const std::size_t numOptions = results.size();
+    if (numOptions == 0) {
+        return 0.0F;
+    }
+
+    // Inputs and outputs share one allocation to minimize CUDA allocation
+    // overhead while retaining separately aligned contiguous regions.
+    DeviceBuffer<double> deviceStorage(packedOptions.size() + numOptions);
+    double* const deviceInputs = deviceStorage.get();
+    double* const deviceResults = deviceInputs + packedOptions.size();
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceInputs, packedOptions.data(),
+                         packedOptions.size() * sizeof(double),
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+              "input transfer");
+
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDevice(&device), "device query");
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device),
+              "device-properties query");
+
+    const std::size_t requiredBlocks =
+        numOptions / kThreadsPerBlock +
+        (numOptions % kThreadsPerBlock != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+    const std::size_t maxBlocks =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(properties.maxGridSize[0]);
+    const int blockCount =
+        static_cast<int>(std::min(requiredBlocks, maxBlocks));
+
+    // Force lazy module loading before the timed region so the reported
+    // computation time measures kernel work rather than one-time setup.
+    cudaFuncAttributes kernelAttributes{};
+    cudaCheck(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&kernelAttributes, blackScholesKernel),
+              "pricing-kernel initialization");
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start-event creation");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop-event creation");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(start), "start-event recording");
+
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blockCount, kThreadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceInputs, deviceResults, numOptions);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "pricing-kernel launch");
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(stop), "stop-event recording");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "pricing-kernel execution");
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop),
+              "kernel timing");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(start), "start-event destruction");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "stop-event destruction");
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                         numOptions * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "result transfer");
+    return elapsedMilliseconds;
+}

Implemented an unconditional CUDA version while preserving the `black_scholes` executable and CLI.

Key changes:

- GPU grid-stride kernel with one thread per option.
- Coalesced structure-of-arrays memory layout.
- Branch-minimized call/put pricing in double precision.
- Native GPU architecture and optimized CUDA compilation.
- CUDA event timing and explicit error handling.
- No CPU fallback or new dependencies/files.

+void cudaCheck(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                     cudaGetErrorString(status));

+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const std::size_t count) {
+        if (count != 0) {
+            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 count * sizeof(T)),
+                      "device allocation");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    [[nodiscard]] T* get() noexcept { return data_; }
+    [[nodiscard]] const T* get() const noexcept { return data_; }
+
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};

+// Standard normal cumulative distribution function.  Keeping this operation
+// in double precision preserves the numerical behavior of the CPU benchmark.
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * kInvSqrtTwo));
 }

+// One thread prices one option in the common case.  The grid-stride loop also
+// supports data sets larger than the device's maximum one-dimensional grid.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(kThreadsPerBlock)
+void blackScholesKernel(const double* __restrict__ inputs,
+                        double* __restrict__ prices,
+                        const std::size_t numOptions) {
+    const double* const signs = inputs + SIGN * numOptions;
+    const double* const strikes = inputs + STRIKE * numOptions;
+    const double* const spots = inputs + SPOT * numOptions;
+    const double* const dividendYields = inputs + DIVIDEND_YIELD * numOptions;
+    const double* const riskFreeRates = inputs + RISK_FREE_RATE * numOptions;
+    const double* const times = inputs + TIME_TO_MATURITY * numOptions;
+    const double* const volatilities = inputs + VOLATILITY * numOptions;
+
+    const std::size_t first =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const std::size_t stride =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;
+
+    for (std::size_t i = first; i < numOptions; i += stride) {
+        const double time = times[i];
+        const double volatility = volatilities[i];
+
+        if (time <= 0.0 || volatility <= 0.0) {
+            prices[i] = 0.0;
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const double spot = spots[i];
+        const double strike = strikes[i];
+        const double dividendYield = dividendYields[i];
+        const double riskFreeRate = riskFreeRates[i];
+        const double sqrtTime = sqrt(time);
+        const double volatilitySqrtTime = volatility * sqrtTime;
+        const double d1 =
+            (log(spot / strike) +
+             (riskFreeRate - dividendYield +
+              0.5 * volatility * volatility) *
+                 time) /
+            volatilitySqrtTime;
+        const double d2 = d1 - volatilitySqrtTime;
+
+        // sign is +1 for calls and -1 for puts.  This form removes divergent
+        // call/put branches while evaluating the same two normal CDFs.
+        const double sign = signs[i];
+        const double discountedSpot = spot * exp(-dividendYield * time);
+        const double discountedStrike = strike * exp(-riskFreeRate * time);
+        prices[i] =
+            sign * (discountedSpot * cumulativeNormalDevice(sign * d1) -
+                    discountedStrike * cumulativeNormalDevice(sign * d2));
     }
-    
-    return price;
 }

+// Convert the host-friendly array of structures to a device-friendly
+// structure of arrays.  Every warp consequently issues coalesced loads for
+// each field used by the pricing kernel.
+void packOptions(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,
+                 std::vector<double>& packedOptions) {
+    const std::size_t numOptions = options.size();
+    packedOptions.resize(kInputFieldCount * numOptions);
+
+    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
+        const OptionInput& option = options[i];
+        packedOptions[SIGN * numOptions + i] =
+            option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+        packedOptions[STRIKE * numOptions + i] = option.strike;
+        packedOptions[SPOT * numOptions + i] = option.spot;
+        packedOptions[DIVIDEND_YIELD * numOptions + i] = option.q;
+        packedOptions[RISK_FREE_RATE * numOptions + i] = option.r;
+        packedOptions[TIME_TO_MATURITY * numOptions + i] = option.t;
+        packedOptions[VOLATILITY * numOptions + i] = option.vol;
+    }
+}
+
+float priceOptionsCuda(const std::vector<double>& packedOptions,
+                       std::vector<double>& results) {
+    const std::size_t numOptions = results.size();
+    if (numOptions == 0) {
+        return 0.0F;
+    }
+
+    // Inputs and outputs share one allocation to minimize CUDA allocation
+    // overhead while retaining separately aligned contiguous regions.
+    DeviceBuffer<double> deviceStorage(packedOptions.size() + numOptions);
+    double* const deviceInputs = deviceStorage.get();
+    double* const deviceResults = deviceInputs + packedOptions.size();
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(deviceInputs, packedOptions.data(),
+                         packedOptions.size() * sizeof(double),
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+              "input transfer");
+
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDevice(&device), "device query");
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device),
+              "device-properties query");
+
+    const std::size_t requiredBlocks =
+        numOptions / kThreadsPerBlock +
+        (numOptions % kThreadsPerBlock != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+    const std::size_t maxBlocks =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(properties.maxGridSize[0]);
+    const int blockCount =
+        static_cast<int>(std::min(requiredBlocks, maxBlocks));
+
+    // Force lazy module loading before the timed region so the reported
+    // computation time measures kernel work rather than one-time setup.
+    cudaFuncAttributes kernelAttributes{};
+    cudaCheck(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&kernelAttributes, blackScholesKernel),
+              "pricing-kernel initialization");
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start-event creation");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop-event creation");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(start), "start-event recording");
+
+    blackScholesKernel<<<blockCount, kThreadsPerBlock>>>(
+        deviceInputs, deviceResults, numOptions);
+    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "pricing-kernel launch");
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventRecord(stop), "stop-event recording");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "pricing-kernel execution");
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop),
+              "kernel timing");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(start), "start-event destruction");
+    cudaCheck(cudaEventDestroy(stop), "stop-event destruction");
+
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(results.data(), deviceResults,
+                         numOptions * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "result transfer");
+    return elapsedMilliseconds;
+}

Key changes:

- GPU grid-stride kernel with one thread per option.
- Coalesced structure-of-arrays memory layout.
- Branch-minimized call/put pricing in double precision.
- Native GPU architecture and optimized CUDA compilation.
- CUDA event timing and explicit error handling.
- No CPU fallback or new dependencies/files.
